<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p><a href="relations-099.md#BWP-R2-01091">BWP-R2-01091</a></p></dd><dt>Source property</dt><dd><p><a href="../property-profiles/properties-014.md#EWM-EWM-062">EWM:EWM-062</a></p></dd><dt>Target property</dt><dd><p><a href="../property-profiles/properties-002.md#EBPM-EBPM-015">EBPM:EBPM-015</a></p></dd><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>CONSTRAINS</p></dd><dt>Directionality</dt><dd><p>DIRECTED</p></dd><dt>Semantic basis</dt><dd><p>Native returning output: Invalid or unsubstantiated proposed actions can be rejected without relying on the same model’s assertion of safety. Keep a traceable connection between a decision model and the process consuming its result.</p></dd><dt>Origin rule</dt><dd><p>BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-015-EWM062-return</p></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>An identified actor supplies the actual applicable mandate, constraint, interface agreement or discretion boundary for the receiving operation. Mere input consumption, a hypothetical requested change, or a silently reversed arrow does not meet this guard.</p></dd><dt>Composition consequence</dt><dd><p>The delivered result changes the adequacy or stated scope of the source account/use. ## BWP-R2-01112 — EPM:EPM-008 → EBPM:EBPM-016

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p><a href="relations-099.md#BWP-R2-01112">BWP-R2-01112</a></p></dd><dt>Source property</dt><dd><p><a href="../property-profiles/properties-016.md#EPM-EPM-008">EPM:EPM-008</a></p></dd><dt>Target property</dt><dd><p><a href="../property-profiles/properties-002.md#EBPM-EBPM-016">EBPM:EBPM-016</a></p></dd><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>CONSTRAINS</p></dd><dt>Directionality</dt><dd><p>DIRECTED</p></dd><dt>Semantic basis</dt><dd><p>Native returning output: Reported denominators correspond to the declared unit, and material exclusions and generalisation limits are explicit. Communication: Exchange event definitions, source examples, extraction rules and unresolved recording ambiguities between the analyst and source/domain custodians. Specific receiving decision: The consumer accepts or rejects the bounded population claim; the analyst cannot expand it by reporting more decimal places. Its actually delivered requirement, checked discrepancy or bounded result constrains this native function: Select support according to what can be known in advance: goals and information needs, mandatory constraints, planned tasks or a stable flow. Allow structured sub-processes inside knowledge work and avoid making flexibility equivalent to the absence of guidance.</p></dd><dt>Origin rule</dt><dd><p>BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-016-EPM008-return</p></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>The receiver uses the actual selected route, supported behaviour, work choice or independently checked proposed action as a constraint, context or observed action for this same work.
